63: def login_required(fn):
64:     @wraps(fn)
65:     def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
66:         if not session.get("user_id"):
67:             return redirect(url_for("auth.login"))

4166:   // Cap: notOk + hold cannot exceed available
4167:   if (notOk + hold > available) {
4168:     if (changedFieldId === "operator-hold-qty") {
4169:       hold = Math.max(0, available - notOk);
4170:     } else {
4171:       notOk = Math.max(0, available - hold);
4172:     }
4173:   }
4174: 
4175:   // Cap ok: cannot exceed available - notOk - hold
4176:   const maxOk = Math.max(0, available - notOk - hold);
4177:   if (ok > maxOk) ok = maxOk;

4178:

317: function highlightRolePill(role) {
318:   ["admin", "supervisor", "operator"].forEach(r => {
319:     document.getElementById(`role-pill-${r}`).classList.toggle("selected", r === role);
320:   });
321: }

323: function selectRolePill(newRole) {
324:   const u = allUsers.find(u => u.id === viewingUserId);
325:   if (!u || newRole === u.role) return; // clicking the current role is a no-op
326:   pendingRoleChange = { fromRole: u.role, toRole: newRole };
327:   highlightRolePill(newRole); // preview the selection visually
